For notational convenience, we define

\displaystyle{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y})\equiv\frac{\partial\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}},\quad{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y})\equiv\frac{\partial^{2}\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}},\quad{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y})\equiv{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y})+\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y})\right\}^{\otimes 2},

\displaystyle Q_{1}(u)\equiv\frac{d\mbox{log}f_{U}(u)}{du},\quad Q_{2}(u)\equiv\frac{d^{2}\mbox{log}f_{U}(u)}{du^{2}}.

Furthermore, for any real, vector or matrix valued function

{\bf a}({\bf y})
, we define

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\{{\bf a}({\bf Y})\mid\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\}\equiv\int{\bf a}({\bf y})f_{\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf X}-\widetilde{\bm{\mu}})}({\bf y})d{\bf y},\quad E_{\widehat{p}}\{{\bf a}({\bf Y})\mid\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\}\equiv\int{\bf a}({\bf y})f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}({\bf y})d{\bf y}.

We state the regularity conditions as follows.

A

Finite moment conditions on
{\bf Y}
:

1.

E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{8})<\infty
;

2.

E(1/\|{\bf Y}\|^{3})<\infty
;

3.

E(\mbox{log}\|{\bf Y}\|)<\infty
;

B

Finiteness conditions on expectations with respect to
f_{\bf Y}
:

1.

E\{\|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\|^{4}\}<\infty
;

2.

E\{\|{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\|_{F}^{4}\}<\infty
;

C

Finiteness conditions on expectations with respect to
f_{U}
:

1.

E\{|Q_{1}(U)|^{3}\}<\infty
;

2.

E\{|Q_{2}(U)|^{2}\}<\infty
;

D

Continuity assumptions with respect to
\widetilde{\bm{\mu}}
and
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}
:

1.

At any
{\bf x}
,
f_{\bf X}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}})

as a function of
(\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}})

is continuous at
({\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})
.

2.

The functions of the form
E_{\widetilde{p}}(\cdot|\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}})
that
are given in (
S.13
), (
S.16
),
(
S.24
), (
S.33
), (
S.34
),
(
S.35
), (
S.37
), (
S.41
),
(
S.42
), (
S.43
), (
S.44
),
(
S.45
), (
S.49
) and (
S.53
)
are continuous functions of
(\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}})
at the
point
({\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})
.

E

Lipschitz conditions for
{\bf S}_{2}
and
Q_{2}
: there
exists a finite constant
L
such that

1.

\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{1})-{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{2})\|\leq L\|{\bf y}_{1}-{\bf y}_{2}\|
for all
{\bf y}_{1},{\bf y}_{2}\in\mathrm{supp}({\bf Y})
;

2.

\|Q_{2}(u_{1})-Q_{2}(u_{2})\|\leq L|u_{1}-u_{2}|

for all
u_{1},u_{2}\in\mathrm{supp}(U)
.

The assumptions
A
-
E
are all related to
boundedness of various expectations
and smoothness or Lipschitz condition of
various functions, hence are very mild overall. In particular, we do not
impose compact support requirement on the variable
{\bf X}
, and we do not
require
{\bf X}
to be bounded away from zero either.
Thus, the Lipschitz condition in Assumption
EE1
need to hold
for all
{\bf y}_{1},{\bf y}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
, and that in Assumption

EE2
need to hold for all
u_{1},u_{2}\geq 0
if the support
of
{\bf Y}

is the entire
p
-dimensional real space.
Assumptions in
B
and
C
are intrinsically
linked because
U=\|{\bf Y}\|
. However, we find that Assumption
B

does not directly imply Assumption

C
unless various other additional conditions are imposed. In view of clarity
and simplicity, we thus retain Assumption

C
. We also point out that Assumptions
B
and

C
are not results from other Assumptions listed.
For example, Assumption
E
is not sufficient to ensure
Assumptions
B
and
C
due to the appearance of
higher moments in the latter Assumptions.
In Lemma

S.2
, we show that
f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})
is
contiguity with respect to
f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}}_{n},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n})
under
Assumption
DD1
, a property that will be used in various
places of the proof. Similar to Assumption
DD1
,
Assumption
DD2
only assumes continuity at a single
point hence these are very mild assumptions.

Interestingly, Assumption
AA2
requires
f_{U}(u)
to go
to zero faster than
u^{2}
as
u\to 0
, thus it excludes
distributions such as normal under

p\leq 3
. Technically, this requirement arises because of the need to
control the average power of the relative errors caused by estimating
the length
U_{i}
’s. Intuitively, Assumption
AA2
requires
that
{\bf Y}
is not concentrated near zero, or equivalently,
{\bf X}
should
not be too concentrated near
{\bm{\mu}}
. This is reasonable because
one aspect of the test is about the directions. The closer a point is to
the center, the harder it is to estimate its direction given that the
center is estimated itself, while at the
center the notion of direction itself degenerates. Thus, Assumption

AA2
limits such cases. In this sense, the exclusion of
difficult cases such as 2 and 3 dimensional normal is likely not due to the
particular test statistic that we construct.

S.5

Basic Lemmas

We now prove two lemmas that will be used throughout the proof of
the theorems.

Lemma
S.1
.

\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}x_{i}\right)^{p}\leq m^{p-1}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}x_{i}^{p}\right).

Proof:
By Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{m}x_{i}\leq\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}x_{i}^{p}\right)^{1/p}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}1^{q}\right)^{1/q}=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}x_{i}^{p}\right)^{1/p}m^{1/q},

where
\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}=1
. Therefore

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}x_{i}\right)^{p}\leq\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}x_{i}^{p}\right)m^{p/q}=\left(\sum_{i=1}^{m}x_{i}^{p}\right)m^{p-1}.

∎

Lemma
S.2
.

Under Assumption
DD1
,
f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})
is contiguous with
respect to
f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}}_{n},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n})
.

Proof:
Let the likelihood ratio be

L_{n}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}},{\bm{\mu}}_{n},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n})\equiv f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})/f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}}_{n},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n})
.
Because

1/L_{n}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}},{\bm{\mu}}_{n},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n})
is a continuous function of
({\bm{\mu}}_{n},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n})

at
({\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

and because
{\bm{\mu}}_{n}\to{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}\to{\bm{\Sigma}}
in probability under

f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})
,
when
n\to\infty
,
1/L_{n}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}},{\bm{\mu}}_{n},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n})
converges to 1 in probability under

f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})
.
Thus, by Le Cam’s first lemma (see, for example, Lemma 6.4 of

van der Vaart 1998
),

f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})
is contiguous with
respect to
f_{\bf X}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}}_{n},{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n})
.
∎

S.6

Proof of Theorem
2

We will divide the proof of Theorem
2
into
several parts. We expand the three centralized non-constant terms in
(
11
) separately: in Section
S.6.2
, we will expand
the term
\widehat{E}_{n_{2}}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}
; in Section

S.6.3
, we will expand the term
-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})
; in Section
S.6.4
, we will expand the term
\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})-H(U)
. For preparation, before exploring their
expansions, we first provide the asymptotic expansions of some basic
functions in Section
S.6.1
.
In addition to
{\bf S}_{1},{\bf S}_{2},{\bf S}_{3}
and
R_{1},R_{2}
, we further define

{\bf A}\equiv{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}
and

{\bf c}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bm{\mu}}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})
. Also note that

E\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\}={\bf 0}
and
E\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\}=-{\bf I}
, which will be
used in the proof.

S.6.1

Basic functions

Asymptotic expansion of
\widehat{\bm{\mu}}-{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}

Define

\displaystyle{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\bf x}-{\bm{\mu}},

\displaystyle{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle({\bf x}-{\bm{\mu}})^{\otimes 2}-{\bm{\Sigma}},

\displaystyle{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mbox{dvec}\{-({\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}+{\bm{\Sigma}}\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2})^{-1}\mbox{vec}({\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\},

where
dvec
is the inverse operation of
vec
.
Then, since
E(\|{\bf X}\|^{2})<\infty
due to
{\bm{\mu}}
and
{\bm{\Sigma}}
being
finite, by Lemma 9.1 of
Li 2018
,
we have

\displaystyle n_{1}^{1/2}(\widehat{\bm{\mu}}-{\bm{\mu}})=n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})=n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}({\bf X}_{j}-{\bm{\mu}}),

(S.2)

\displaystyle n_{1}^{1/2}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-{\bm{\Sigma}})=n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+{\bf r}_{1}=n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\{({\bf X}_{j}-{\bm{\mu}})^{\otimes 2}-{\bm{\Sigma}}\}+{\bf r}_{1},

(S.3)

where
{\bf r}_{1}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2})
, and

\displaystyle n_{1}^{1/2}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2})=n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+{\bf r}_{2}

(S.4)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\mbox{dvec}\{-({\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}+{\bm{\Sigma}}\otimes{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2})^{-1}\mbox{vec}({\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\}+{\bf r}_{2},

where
{\bf r}_{2}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2})
.

Asymptotic expansion of
\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|

For convenience, we also expand
\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|
as follows:

\displaystyle n_{1}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2})|

(S.5)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{1}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\}+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}).

S.6.2

Expanding
\widehat{E}_{n_{2}}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}

S.6.2.1

Asymptotic expansion of
\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}

We first give the asymptotic expansion of
\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}
in terms of

{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\mu}}}
and
{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}
. Under Assumption

AA1
,

E(\|{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}\|^{2})<\infty
,

E(\|{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\Sigma}}\|^{2})<\infty
and

E(\|{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}\|^{2})<\infty
.
Further,

\displaystyle\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}){\bf X}_{i}-(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2})\widehat{\bm{\mu}}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}(\widehat{\bm{\mu}}-{\bm{\mu}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}){\bf X}_{i}-(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}){\bm{\mu}}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}(\widehat{\bm{\mu}}-{\bm{\mu}})+{\bf r}_{3},

where

\displaystyle{\bf r}_{3}=(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2})(\widehat{\bm{\mu}}-{\bm{\mu}})=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}).

So, incorporating (
S.2
) and (
S.4
), we have the expansion

\displaystyle\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}

(S.6)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})({\bf X}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}})-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\}+n_{1}^{-1/2}{\bf r}_{2}({\bf X}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}})+{\bf r}_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\}+{\bf r}_{4}{\bf Y}_{i}+{\bf r}_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bf Y}_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+{\bf r}_{4}{\bf Y}_{i}+{\bf r}_{3},

where
{\bf r}_{4}=n_{1}^{-1/2}{\bf r}_{2}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1})
, and

\displaystyle{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bf Y}_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})={\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}}).

S.6.2.2

Two technical lemmas

Based on (
S.6
), we prove two technical lemmas that will be used several times afterwards.

Lemma
S.3
.

For any integer
k
,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k/2})

when

E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{k})<\infty
.

Proof: Using Lemma
S.1
for
m=4,p=k
in (
S.6
) leads to

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4^{k-1}\left[n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k}+n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\|^{k}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k}\|{\bf r}_{4}\|^{k}+n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf r}_{3}\|^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4^{k-1}\left[\|n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\|^{k}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\|^{k}\left(n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k}\right)+\|n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\|^{k}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\bf r}_{4}\|^{k}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k}+\|{\bf r}_{3}\|^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k/2})O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k/2})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k})O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k/2})

under the Assumption that
E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{k})<\infty
.
∎

Lemma
S.4
.

For general
k,l\in\mathbb{N}
, and a general function
a({\bf y})
,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{l}}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k/2})

when
E(|a({\bf Y})|\|{\bf Y}\|^{k-l})<\infty
and
E(|a({\bf Y})|\|{\bf Y}\|^{-l})<\infty
.

Proof:
Using Lemma
S.1
for
m=4,p=k
in (
S.6
) leads to

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{l}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4^{k-1}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\frac{\|n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{l}}

\displaystyle+4^{k-1}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\frac{\|n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\|^{k}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{l}}

\displaystyle+4^{k-1}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k-l}\|{\bf r}_{4}\|^{k}+4^{k-1}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\frac{1}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{l}}\|{\bf r}_{3}\|^{k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4^{k-1}\|n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\|^{k}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\|^{k}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k-l}

\displaystyle+4^{k-1}\|n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\|^{k}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\frac{1}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{l}}

\displaystyle+4^{k-1}\|{\bf r}_{4}\|^{k}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{k-l}+4^{k-1}\|{\bf r}_{3}\|^{k}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|a({\bf y}_{i})|\frac{1}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{l}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k/2})O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k/2})O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k})O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k})O_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-k/2})

under the assumption that
E(|a({\bf Y})|\|{\bf Y}\|^{k-l})<\infty
,

E(|a({\bf Y})|\|{\bf Y}\|^{-l})<\infty
.∎

S.6.2.3

Asymptotic expansion of
\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}

Main expansion of
\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}

Next, we give the asymptotic expansion of
\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}
based on
(
S.6
). We expand
\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}
as follows:

\displaystyle\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}=\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|=\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|+\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}

(S.7)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{2\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|\{1+(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)/(2\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{2\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}\{1+C_{i}(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)/(2\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{2\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}+C_{i}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|+\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)}{2\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}{2\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{2\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}+\frac{C_{i}}{2}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}+\frac{C_{i}}{4}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{3}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}(\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i})+(\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i})^{\top}{\bf Y}_{i}}{2U_{i}}+\frac{C_{i}}{2}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}+\frac{C_{i}}{4}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{3}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i})^{\top}{\bf Y}_{i}}{U_{i}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}+\frac{C_{i}}{2}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}+\frac{C_{i}}{4}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{3}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i})^{\top}{\bf Y}_{i}}{U_{i}}+S_{i1}+S_{i2},

where

\displaystyle S_{i1}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}+\frac{C_{i}}{2}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|},\quad S_{i2}=\frac{C_{i}}{4}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{3}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}},

and
C_{i}
is a random quantity between 0 and
-4
. This is because

\frac{1}{1+x}=1-\frac{1}{(1+x^{*})^{2}}x
where
x^{*}
is between 0 and

x
, then plug in
x=(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)/(2\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)\geq-1/2
.

Dominating term of
\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}

We first expand the first term in
\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}
. Plugging
(
S.6
) into (
S.7
), we have

\displaystyle\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\frac{{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bf Y}_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}+\frac{{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}{\bf r}_{4}{\bf Y}_{i}}{U_{i}}+{\bf V}_{i}^{\top}{\bf r}_{3}+S_{i1}+S_{i2}

(S.8)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\frac{{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bf Y}_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})}{U_{i}}+S_{i4}+S_{i3}+S_{i1}+S_{i2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\psi_{U_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+S_{i4}+S_{i3}+S_{i1}+S_{i2},

where

\displaystyle S_{i4}=\frac{{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}{\bf r}_{4}{\bf Y}_{i}}{U_{i}}={\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}{\bf r}_{4}{\bf V}_{i},\quad S_{i3}={\bf V}_{i}^{\top}{\bf r}_{3},

and

\displaystyle\psi_{U_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})=\frac{{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bf Y}_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})}{U_{i}}=\frac{{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\}}{U_{i}}.

(S.9)

Upper bound of
|S_{ik}|
in
\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}
for

k=1,\dots,4
.

For the remainder terms
S_{i3}
and
S_{i4}

in (
S.8
), we have

|S_{i4}|\leq\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|\|{\bf r}_{4}\|
,
|S_{i3}|\leq\|{\bf r}_{3}\|
.
Further, for
S_{i1}
and
S_{i2}
, we have

\displaystyle|S_{i1}|\leq\frac{1}{2}\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}+\frac{|C_{i}|}{2}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}\leq\frac{1+|C_{i}|}{2}\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}\leq\frac{5}{2}\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|},

(S.10)

and

\displaystyle|S_{i2}|\leq\frac{|C_{i}|}{4}\frac{|\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\||^{3}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}\leq\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{3}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}.

(S.11)

S.6.2.4

Asymptotic expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}

A Taylor expansion yields

\displaystyle\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})=\frac{\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}}{U_{i}}-R_{i},\quad R_{i}=\int_{U_{i}}^{\widehat{U}_{i}}\frac{\widehat{U}_{i}-t}{t^{2}}dt.

In the second term, we let
t=(1-s)\widehat{U}_{i}+sU_{i}
, then
dt=(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})ds
. Thus,

\displaystyle R_{i}=\int_{U_{i}}^{\widehat{U}_{i}}\frac{\widehat{U}_{i}-t}{t^{2}}dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{0}\frac{\widehat{U}_{i}-(1-s)\widehat{U}_{i}-sU_{i}}{\{(1-s)\widehat{U}_{i}+sU_{i}\}^{2}}(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{s}{\{(1-s)\widehat{U}_{i}+sU_{i}\}^{2}}ds.

Truncation for the remainder term

To overcome the difficulties in
controlling the remainder term
R_{i}
when
\widehat{U}_{i}
is
close to 0, we propose to handle the term
R_{i}
using
truncation on
\widehat{U}_{i}
.
Let the event
\mathcal{E}_{1}
be
\mathcal{E}_{1}=\{\widehat{U}_{i}\geq\epsilon_{n}U_{i},\forall i\in\{n_{1}+1,\ldots,n\}\}
and denote
I(\cdot)
the indicator function.

Note that
\mathcal{E}_{1}
contains the nice cases where
all the
\widehat{U}_{i}
’s are reasonably large compared to
their corresponding
U_{i}
’s. As we will see in the following
derivation, the remainder term can be controlled under
\mathcal{E}_{1}
, as
the extreme values of
\widehat{U}_{i}
’s are ruled out. Regarding the
extreme cases under
\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C}
, we will further show that they are
sufficiently rare
so they are ignorable in probability.

We first discuss the expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}

under
\mathcal{E}_{1}
, that is,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})=n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}}{U_{i}}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})-n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}R_{i}I(\mathcal{E}_{1}).

Remainder term under
\mathcal{E}_{1}

Note that for
a>0
,

\displaystyle\int\frac{x}{(ax+b)^{2}}dx=\frac{1}{a}\int\frac{1}{ax+b}dx-\frac{b}{a}\int\frac{1}{(ax+b)^{2}}dx=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\mbox{log}|ax+b|+\frac{b}{a^{2}}\frac{1}{ax+b}+C.

Noting that
R_{i}
’s are non-negative, we have

\displaystyle R_{i}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I(\mathcal{E}_{1})(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{s}{\{(1-s)\widehat{U}_{i}+sU_{i}\}^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{s}{\{(1-s)\epsilon_{n}U_{i}+sU_{i}\}^{2}}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{s}{\{s(1-\epsilon_{n})U_{i}+\epsilon_{n}U_{i}\}^{2}}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\left[\frac{1}{\{(1-\epsilon_{n})U_{i}\}^{2}}\mbox{log}|s(1-\epsilon_{n})U_{i}+\epsilon_{n}U_{i}|+\frac{\epsilon_{n}U_{i}}{\{(1-\epsilon_{n})U_{i}\}^{2}}\frac{1}{s(1-\epsilon_{n})U_{i}+\epsilon_{n}U_{i}}\right]_{0}^{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\left[\frac{1}{\{(1-\epsilon_{n})U_{i}\}^{2}}\left\{\mbox{log}|U_{i}|-\mbox{log}|\epsilon_{n}U_{i}|\right\}+\frac{\epsilon_{n}U_{i}}{\{(1-\epsilon_{n})U_{i}\}^{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{U_{i}}-\frac{1}{\epsilon_{n}U_{i}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\left[-\frac{\mbox{log}(\epsilon_{n})}{\{(1-\epsilon_{n})U_{i}\}^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon_{n}-1}{\{(1-\epsilon_{n})U_{i}\}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}}{U_{i}^{2}}\left\{\frac{-\mbox{log}(\epsilon_{n})+\epsilon_{n}-1}{(1-\epsilon_{n})^{2}}\right\}.

Let
\epsilon_{n}=n_{1}^{-k}
where
k\geq 2
,
then we further have

\displaystyle R_{i}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}}{U_{i}^{2}}\left\{\frac{-\mbox{log}(\epsilon_{n})+\epsilon_{n}-1}{(1-\epsilon_{n})^{2}}\right\}=\frac{(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}}{U_{i}^{2}}\left\{\frac{k\mbox{log}(n_{1})+n_{1}^{-k}-1}{(1-n_{1}^{-k})^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2k\frac{(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}}{U_{i}^{2}}\mbox{log}(n_{1})

for sufficiently large
n
.
By Lemma
S.4
,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{(\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i})^{2}}{U_{i}^{2}}=n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|-\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|)^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}=O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1})

under
E(1/\|{\bf Y}\|^{2})<\infty
, which is implied by Assumption
AA2
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}R_{i}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}2k\frac{(U_{i}-\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}}{U_{i}^{2}}\mbox{log}(n_{1})=O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1})).

Dominating term under
\mathcal{E}_{1}

Note that

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\widehat{U}_{i}-U_{i}}{U_{i}}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})+O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\psi_{U_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+S_{i4}+S_{i3}+S_{i1}+S_{i2}}{U_{i}}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})+O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\frac{1}{U_{i}}\psi_{U_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})I(\mathcal{E}_{1})+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{S_{i4}}{U_{i}}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{S_{i3}}{U_{i}}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{S_{i1}}{U_{i}}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{S_{i2}}{U_{i}}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})+O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1})).

For the first summation, we have

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\frac{1}{U_{i}}\psi_{U_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

(S.12)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\frac{1}{U_{i}^{2}}{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bf Y}_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\frac{1}{U_{i}^{2}}{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})({\bf X}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}})-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\frac{1}{U_{i}^{2}}{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\left\{n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{{\bf Y}_{i}{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}}{U_{i}^{2}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right\}\left\{n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{U_{i}^{2}}{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}\right\}\right].

Note that, under Assumption
AA2
, the first term of (
S.12
) is

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\left\{n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{{\bf Y}_{i}{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}}{U_{i}^{2}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\left\{E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right)+O_{p}(n_{2}^{-1/2})\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right)\right]+n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left\{O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}O_{p}(n_{2}^{-1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right)\right]+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}),

and the second term of (
S.12
) is

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right\}\left\{n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{U_{i}^{2}}{\bf Y}_{i}^{\top}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right\}\left\{E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right)+O_{p}(n_{2}^{-1/2})\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right\}E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right)\right]+n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left\{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2})O_{p}(n_{2}^{-1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right\}E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right)\right]+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}).

Plugging back into (
S.12
), we have

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\frac{1}{U_{i}}\psi_{U_{i}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right){\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right]

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}).

Other ignorable terms under
\mathcal{E}_{1}

We now analyze the four terms related to
S_{i1}
,
S_{i2}
,
S_{i3}
,

S_{i4}
. Note that, under
E(1/\|{\bf Y}\|^{2})<\infty
, which is implied by
Assumption
AA2
, using (
S.10
) and Lemma
S.4
, we have

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{S_{i1}}{U_{i}}\right|\leq n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\left|S_{i1}\right|}{U_{i}}\leq n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{5}{2}\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}),

Similarly, under
E(1/\|{\bf Y}\|^{3})<\infty
, which is also implied by
Assumption
AA2
, using (
S.11
) and Lemma
S.4
, we have

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{S_{i2}}{U_{i}}\right|\leq n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\left|S_{i2}\right|}{U_{i}}\leq n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{3}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{3}}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-3/2}).

Furthermore, under
E(1/\|{\bf Y}\|)<\infty
, which is also implied by
Assumption
AA2
, Lemma
S.4
implies that

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{S_{i3}}{U_{i}}\right|\leq n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\left|S_{i3}\right|}{U_{i}}\leq n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\|{\bf r}_{3}\|}{U_{i}}=\|{\bf r}_{3}\|n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1})

The remaining term can also be bounded through the following:

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{S_{i4}}{U_{i}}\right|\leq n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\left|S_{i4}\right|}{U_{i}}\leq n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|\|{\bf r}_{4}\|}{U_{i}}=\|{\bf r}_{4}\|=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}).

Analysis of
\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C}

We now show that
the probability for
the event
\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C}
to happen is ignorable
by estimating the rate of
I(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C})
as follows.

\displaystyle I(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I(\exists i\in\{n_{1}+1,\ldots,n\}\mbox{ s.t. }\widehat{U}_{i}\leq\epsilon_{n}U_{i})\leq\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}I(\widehat{U}_{i}\leq\epsilon_{n}U_{i})=\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}I(\epsilon_{n}U_{i}/\widehat{U}_{i}\geq 1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\epsilon_{n}\frac{U_{i}}{\widehat{U}_{i}}=\epsilon_{n}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|}=\epsilon_{n}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\|{\bf A}(\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf c})\|}{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|}\leq\epsilon_{n}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\|{\bf A}\|(\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|+\|{\bf c}\|)}{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\epsilon_{n}\|{\bf A}\|\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left\{1+\frac{\|{\bf c}\|}{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|}\right\}=\epsilon_{n}\|{\bf A}\|\left\{n_{2}+\|{\bf c}\|\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}\|}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}\epsilon_{n}\|{\bf A}\|+n_{2}\epsilon_{n}\|{\bf A}\|\|{\bf c}\|E_{\widehat{p}}\left(1/\|\widehat{\bf Y}\|\mid\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right)+n_{2}\epsilon_{n}\|{\bf A}\|\|{\bf c}\|o_{\widehat{p}}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}\epsilon_{n}\|{\bf A}\|+n_{2}\epsilon_{n}\|{\bf A}\|\|{\bf c}\|E_{\widehat{p}}\left(1/\|\widehat{\bf Y}\|\mid\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right)+n_{2}\epsilon_{n}\|{\bf A}\|\|{\bf c}\|o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{2}\epsilon_{n})+n_{2}\epsilon_{n}o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{2}\epsilon_{n}),

where the fourth last equality uses large number theory, the
third last equality uses contiguity under Assumption
DD1
, the
second last equality uses Assumption
DD2
with respect to

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\left(1/\|\widetilde{\bf Y}\|\mid\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right)

(S.13)

and

E(1/\|{\bf Y}\|)<\infty
, which is implied by Assumption
AA2
.
Specifically, let
m_{1}(\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}})\equiv E_{\widetilde{p}}\{\|\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf Y}+{\bm{\mu}}-\widetilde{\bm{\mu}})\|^{-1}\mid\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\}
. As
n_{1}\to\infty
, we
have
\widehat{\bm{\mu}}\to{\bm{\mu}}
and
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\to{\bm{\Sigma}}
in probability, so
by Slutsky’s theorem and continuous mapping theorem,

m_{1}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}},\widehat{\bm{\mu}})\to m_{1}({\bm{\Sigma}},{\bm{\mu}})
in probability,
indicating that
m_{1}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}},\widehat{\bm{\mu}})=O_{p}(1)
.

Hence,
when
\epsilon_{n}=n_{1}^{-k}
, we have

\displaystyle I(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{2}n_{1}^{-k}).

(S.14)

Summary of results under
\mathcal{E}_{1}

Based on the discussions under
\mathcal{E}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})

(S.15)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left(\frac{{\bf Y}^{\top}}{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}}\right){\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right]I(\mathcal{E}_{1})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2})I(\mathcal{E}_{1})+O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1})I(\mathcal{E}_{1})+O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right]I(\mathcal{E}_{1})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1})).

Inserting (
S.14
) into (
S.15
), we can obtain the
final expansion under
\mathcal{E}_{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right]I(\mathcal{E}_{1})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right]\{1-I(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C})\}

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right]

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{1/2}O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2})O_{p}(n_{2}n_{1}^{-k})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right]

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k-1/2})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1})).

Result under
\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C}

In contrast, under
\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C}
,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}I(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\left[E_{\widehat{p}}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})|\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\}-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}+o_{\widehat{p}}(1)+o_{p}(1)\right]I(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}O_{p}(1)O_{p}(n_{2}n_{1}^{-k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}).

under Assumptions
AA3
,
DD1
,
DD2
with respect to

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\{\mbox{log}(\widetilde{U})|\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\}.

(S.16)

Final expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}

Combining the above results, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}

(S.17)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}I(\mathcal{E}_{1})+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}I(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{C})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{\bm{\mu}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}})\right]

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1})+n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bf y}_{j}\right]

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1})+n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}).

S.6.2.5

Final result for
\widehat{E}_{n_{2}}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}

By (
S.17
),

\displaystyle\widehat{E}_{n_{2}}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}=n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}\}

(S.18)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}(U_{i})\}+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(U_{i})-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bf y}_{j}\right]

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(U_{i})-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}\}+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1})+n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}).

S.6.3

Expanding
-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})

S.6.3.1

Preparations

We first give two equivalent representations of
{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}
,
which will be used later. Note that

\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}=\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf X}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})=\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf Y}_{i}+{\bm{\mu}}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})={\bf A}^{-1}{\bf Y}_{i}+{\bf c}
.
We have

\displaystyle({\bf A}-{\bf I})\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf A}{\bf c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}-{\bf I})\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bm{\mu}}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bm{\mu}}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}})-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i},

and

\displaystyle({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf I}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}})-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bm{\mu}}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}})-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}})-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bm{\mu}}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}})-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bf x}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}.

We also link
f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}({\bf y})
to
f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})
by Taylor expansion as follows.

\displaystyle f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}({\bf y})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f_{\bf Y}\{{\bf A}({\bf y}-{\bf c})\}|\det({\bf A})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f_{\bf Y}\{{\bf y}+({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}|\det({\bf A})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})|\det({\bf A})|+\frac{\partial f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}|\det({\bf A})|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}^{\top}\frac{\partial^{2}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}^{*})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}|\det({\bf A})|,

where
{\bf y}^{*}
is between
{\bf y}
and
\widehat{\bf y}
. Similar operations can be
done on
\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}({\bf y})
as follows.

\displaystyle\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}({\bf y})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}\{{\bf y}+({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}+\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})+\frac{\partial\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}^{\top}\frac{\partial^{2}\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}^{*})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}+\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})+{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y})\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}^{*})\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}+\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|,

where
{\bf y}^{*}
is also between
{\bf y}
and
\widehat{\bf y}
.

S.6.3.2

Asymptotic expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\}

Main expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\}

Under Assumptions
DD2
with respect to (
S.24
)
(given near the end of this subsection),
EE1
,

E(\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf Y})\|_{2}^{2})<\infty
(implied by Assumptions
BB1
and

BB2
) and
E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{4})<\infty
(implied by Assumption

AA1
), we have the following expansion:

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\}

(S.19)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}{\bf S}_{1}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})^{\top}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|

\displaystyle+{n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{2}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i}^{*})\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}{\bf S}_{1}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})^{\top}\{({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}\}+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+{O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y}_{i})^{\top}\{({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}\}+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle-{n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}\}^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i}^{*})\{({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y}_{i})^{\top}\{({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}\}+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+{O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})}.

In the above equation, both
\widehat{\bf y}_{i}^{*}
and
{\bf y}_{i}^{*}
are random
between
{\bf y}_{i}
and
\widehat{\bf y}_{i}
. Note that we absorb two ignorable terms
into
O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})
in the derivation of (
S.19
)
under Assumptions
DD2
,
EE1
,

E(\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf Y})\|_{2}^{2})<\infty
and
E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{4})<\infty
,
respectively in obtaining the second equality and the last equality, which will be
verified later.

Continuing from the results in (
S.19
), we insert the form
of
{\bf A}
and
{\bf c}
and get

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\}

(S.20)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y}_{i})^{\top}\{({\bf I}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}){\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}({\bm{\mu}}-\widehat{\bm{\mu}})\}+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y}_{i})^{\top}\left\{-n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf y}_{i}+n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}\right\}+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right]+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left\{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})

under
E(\|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\|^{2})<\infty
and
E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{2})<\infty
, which are
implied by Assumptions
AA1
and
BB1
.

Verification of two ignorable terms in (
S.19
)

To see that the results in (
S.19
) hold under Assumptions
DD2
,
EE1
,
E(\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf Y})\|_{2}^{2})<\infty
and
E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{4})<\infty
,
let

\displaystyle{\bf D}_{i}={\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i}^{*})-{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i})=\frac{\partial^{2}\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i}^{*})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}-\frac{\partial^{2}\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}.

We note that
\|{\bf D}_{i}\|_{2}\leq L\|{\bf y}_{i}^{*}-{\bf y}_{i}\|\leq L\|\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf y}_{i}\|
. Thus,

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}\}^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i}^{*})\{({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}\}\right|

(S.21)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left|({\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i})^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i})({\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\right|+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left|({\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i})^{\top}{\bf D}_{i}({\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{2}\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i})\|_{2}+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{2}\|{\bf D}_{i}\|_{2}

The first term in (
S.21
) can be further bounded as follows:

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{2}\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\left\{\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{4}\right\}^{1/2}\left\{\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i})\|_{2}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\left\{O_{p}(n_{1}^{-2}n_{2})\right\}^{1/2}\left\{O_{p}(n_{2})\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}),

where the second last equality follows from Lemma
S.3
and

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i})\|_{2}^{2}=E\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf Y})\|_{2}^{2}+o_{p}(1)=O_{p}(1).

The second term in (
S.21
) is straightforward using Lemma
S.3
:

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{2}\|{\bf D}_{i}\|_{2}\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}L\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{3}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-3/2}n_{2}^{1/2}).

Plugging back into (
S.21
), we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}\}^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i}^{*})\{({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}\}

(S.22)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-3/2}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}).

Similarly,
let

\displaystyle\widehat{\bf D}_{i}={\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i}^{*})-{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})=\frac{\partial^{2}\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i}^{*})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}-\frac{\partial^{2}\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}.

Also note that
\|\widehat{\bf D}_{i}\|_{2}\leq L\|\widehat{\bf y}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|\leq L\|\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf y}_{i}\|
, so we get

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i}^{*})\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}\right|

(S.23)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left|({\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i})^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})({\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\right|+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left|({\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i})^{\top}\widehat{\bf D}_{i}({\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{2}\|{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\|_{2}+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{2}\|\widehat{\bf D}_{i}\|_{2}

Similarly, the first term in (
S.23
) can be further bounded as follows:

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{2}\|{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\left\{\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{4}\right\}^{1/2}\left\{\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\|_{2}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\left\{O_{p}(n_{1}^{-2}n_{2})\right\}^{1/2}\left\{O_{p}(n_{2})\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}),

where the second last equality follows from Lemma
S.3
and

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\|_{2}^{2}=E_{\widehat{p}}\{\|{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf Y})\|_{2}^{2}|\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\}+o_{p}(1)=E\|{\bf S}_{2}({\bf Y})\|_{2}^{2}+o_{p}(1)=O_{p}(1),

where we use Assumption
DD2
with respect to

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\{\|{\bf S}_{2}(\widetilde{\bf Y})\|_{2}^{2}|\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\}

(S.24)

and continuous mapping
theorem. Same as the previous term,
the second term in (
S.23
) is straightforward using Lemma
S.3
:

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{2}\|\widehat{\bf D}_{i}\|_{2}\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}L\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}\|^{3}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-3/2}n_{2}^{1/2}).

Plugging back into (
S.23
), we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i}^{*})\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}

(S.25)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-3/2}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}).

Therefore, (
S.22
) and (
S.25
) verify why we can
absorb the two terms in (
S.19
) into

O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})
.

S.6.3.3

Asymptotic expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})\}

On the other hand, we expand the term with respect to

f_{\bf Y}
as follows:

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})\}

(S.26)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y}_{i})^{\top}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf y}_{i})+\frac{1}{2}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf y}_{i})^{\top}{\bf S}_{2}({\bf y}_{i}^{*})(\widehat{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf y}_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left[{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y}_{i})^{\top}\left\{n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bf Y}_{i}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+{\bf r}_{4}{\bf y}_{i}+{\bf r}_{3}\right\}\right]+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left[{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y}_{i})^{\top}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf y}_{j}\}\right]+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\right\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right]

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}E\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\right\}{\bf y}_{j}+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left\{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2}).

In the second
equality in (
S.26
), we use

({\bf I}-{\bf A}^{-1}){\bf y}_{i}-{\bf c}={\bf y}_{i}-\widehat{\bf y}_{i}
and the result in
(
S.22
). In the fourth and fifth equalities in
(
S.26
), we use
E(|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}{\bf Y}|^{2})\leq\{E(\|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\|^{4})\}^{1/2}\{E(\|{\bf Y}|^{4})\}^{1/2}<\infty
,

E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{4})<\infty
and
E(\|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\|^{4})<\infty
, which are implied
by Assumptions
AA1
and
BB1
. In the last equality,
we use
E\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\}={\bf 0}
and
E\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\}=-{\bf I}
.

S.6.3.4

Asymptotic expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})\}

Combining (
S.20
) and (
S.26
), we obtain

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})\}

(S.27)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\}+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right]+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right]+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2}).

S.6.3.5

Final result for
-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})

By (
S.27
) and Section 4 of
Berrett et al. 2019
, we give the final
asymptotic expansion of
-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})
as follows:

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\{-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})\}

(S.28)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left\{\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})\right\}+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}(\widehat{\bf y}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+H({\bf Y})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{\widehat{p}}(1)+n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+H({\bf Y})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+H({\bf Y})\}+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})+o_{p}(1),

where the last equality also uses contiguity, which is implied by
Assumption
DD1
.
Plugging (
S.5
) into (
S.28
), we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\{-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})\}

(S.29)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\}+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+H({\bf Y})\}+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\}+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+H({\bf Y})\}

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})+o_{p}(1).

S.6.4

Expanding
\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})-H(U)

S.6.4.1

Asymptotic expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})\}

Expansion of a single
f_{\widehat{U}}(u)
and
\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(u)

We now proceed to analyze
f_{\widehat{U}}(u)
.
Note that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}=\left[\frac{\partial^{2}\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}+\left\{\frac{\partial\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}}\right\}^{\otimes 2}\right]f_{{\bf Y}}({\bf y}).

Recall the notation of
{\bf S}_{1}({\bf y}),{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y})
, then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}}={\bf S}_{1}({\bf y})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}),\quad\frac{\partial^{2}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}={\bf S}_{3}({\bf y})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}).

We first link
f_{\widehat{U}}(u)
to
f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})
as follows.
Denote
{\bf y}_{t}={\bf y}+t\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}
, which is a function of
{\bf y}
and
t
.

\displaystyle f_{\widehat{U}}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}f_{\widehat{\bf Y}}({\bf y})d{\bf y}

(S.30)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\det({\bf A})|\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}f_{{\bf Y}}\{{\bf y}+({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}d{\bf y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\det({\bf A})|\left[\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})d{\bf y}+\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\frac{\partial f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}d{\bf y}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}^{\top}\frac{\partial^{2}f_{\bf Y}({\bf s})}{\partial{\bf s}\partial{\bf s}^{\top}}\big|_{{\bf s}={\bf y}_{t}}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}(1-t)dtd{\bf y}\right].

Note that the first term in the bracket is exactly
f_{U}(u)
. The
second term can be written as

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\frac{\partial f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}{\partial{\bf y}^{\top}}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}d{\bf y}=\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})d{\bf y}.

Also, we expand the third term into three parts as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}^{\top}\frac{\partial^{2}f_{\bf Y}({\bf s})}{\partial{\bf s}\partial{\bf s}^{\top}}\big|_{{\bf s}={\bf y}_{t}}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}(1-t)dtd{\bf y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}{\bf y}^{\top}({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}(1-t)dtd{\bf y}

\displaystyle+\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})({\bf A}{\bf c})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}

\displaystyle-2\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}(1-t)dtd{\bf y}.

We consider the three parts one by one. For the first part, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}{\bf y}^{\top}({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}(1-t)dtd{\bf y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\left[\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}[\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}{\bf B}_{1}(u)],

where

\displaystyle{\bf B}_{1}(u)=\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}.

For the second part, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})({\bf A}{\bf c})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}({\bf A}{\bf c})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{2}(u)({\bf A}{\bf c}),

where

\displaystyle{\bf B}_{2}(u)=\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}.

For the third part, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}(1-t)dtd{\bf y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}\left\{{\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})\mbox{vec}({\bf A}-{\bf I})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}\left\{{\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}\{\mbox{vec}({\bf A}-{\bf I})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{3}(u)\mbox{vec}({\bf A}-{\bf I}),

where

\displaystyle{\bf B}_{3}(u)=\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}\int_{0}^{1}\left\{{\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}.

Inserting all terms back into (
S.30
), we have

\displaystyle f_{\widehat{U}}(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\det({\bf A})|\left(f_{U}(u)+\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})d{\bf y}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.{\rm tr}[\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}{\bf B}_{1}(u)]+({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{2}(u)({\bf A}{\bf c})-2({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{3}(u)\mbox{vec}({\bf A}-{\bf I})\right).

Let

\displaystyle B(u)=\frac{1}{f_{U}(u)}\left[{\rm tr}\{[({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}]{\bf B}_{1}(u)\}+({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{2}(u)({\bf A}{\bf c})-2({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{3}(u)\mbox{vec}({\bf A}-{\bf I})\right]

and

\displaystyle D(u)=\frac{1}{f_{U}(u)}\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})d{\bf y}.

Further let

\displaystyle\Delta(u)=D(u)+B(u),

and then

\displaystyle f_{\widehat{U}}(u)=|\det({\bf A})|f_{U}(u)\{1+\Delta(u)\}.

Hence,
1+\Delta(u)\geq 0
.

Main expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{u}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{u}_{i})\}

Using Taylor expansion, we have

\displaystyle\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(u)=\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+\mbox{log}\{f_{U}(u)\}+\mbox{log}\{1+\Delta(u)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+\mbox{log}\{f_{U}(u)\}+\Delta(u)-\int_{0}^{\Delta(u)}\frac{\Delta(u)-s}{(1+s)^{2}}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+\mbox{log}\{f_{U}(u)\}+\Delta(u)-\Delta(u)^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{r}{\{(1-r)(\Delta(u)+1)+r\}^{2}}dr.

Therefore,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{u}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{u}_{i})\}

(S.31)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{u}_{i})-n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}R_{i},

where

\displaystyle R_{i}=\Delta(\widehat{u}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{r}{\{(1-r)(\Delta(\widehat{u}_{i})+1)+r\}^{2}}dr.

(S.32)

Expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})

In the following proof, we first give the expansion of the linear term
in the Taylor expansion,
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})
, by analyzing
B(u)

and
D(u)
separately. We start from expanding
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}B(\widehat{U}_{i})
.

We first analyze
{\bf B}_{1}(u)
,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}{\rm tr}[\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}{\bf B}_{1}(\widehat{U}_{i})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}[\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=\widehat{U}_{i}}\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})(1-t)dtd{\bf y}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\left(\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}E\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\Big|U=\widehat{U}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\left\{\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}n_{2}^{1/2}E_{\widehat{p}}\left(E\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\Big|\widehat{U},\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\Big|\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+{\rm tr}\left[\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}n_{2}^{1/2}o_{\widehat{p}}(1)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\left([({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}]n_{2}^{1/2}E_{\widehat{p}}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\Big|\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\right)+o_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})\left\{E\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\right]+o_{p}(1)\right\}+o_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})E\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\right\}({\bf Y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf Y}^{\top})(1-t)dt\right]+o_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})E\left[\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\right\}({\bf Y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf Y}^{\top})\right]+o_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}),

where the fifth last equality uses contiguity under Assumption
DD1
, the fourth last
equation uses Assumption
DD2
with respect to

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\Big|\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]

(S.33)

and the last
equation uses
E[\mbox{vec}\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\}({\bf Y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf Y}^{\top})]<\infty
,
which is equivalent to
E\{{\bf Y}^{\top}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y}){\bf Y}\}<\infty
, and is implied by Assumptions
AA1
,
BB1
and
BB2
.

Similarly, for
{\bf B}_{2}(u)
, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{2}(\widehat{U}_{i})({\bf A}{\bf c})=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

under Assumption
DD2
with respect to

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\left\{\int_{0}^{1}{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\Big|\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right\}

(S.34)

and
E\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\}<\infty
implied by Assumptions
BB1
and
BB2
.

Also similarly, for
{\bf B}_{3}(u)
, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{3}(\widehat{U}_{i})\mbox{vec}({\bf A}-{\bf I})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

under Assumption
DD2
with respect to

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\left\{{\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\Big|\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]

(S.35)

and

E\{{\bf Y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\}<\infty
implied by Assumptions
AA1
,
BB1
and
BB2
.

Therefore, under Assumptions
DD2
with respect to (
S.33
), (
S.34
), (
S.35
), Assumptions
AA1
,
BB1
and
BB2
, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}B(\widehat{U}_{i})=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}).

(S.36)

We finally consider
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}D(\widehat{U}_{i})
. Note that

\displaystyle D(u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{f_{U}(u)}\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=u}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})d{\bf y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\left[{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I}){\bf Y}-{\bf A}{\bf c}\}|U=u,\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\left[({\bf A}-{\bf I})E\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|U=u\right\}-({\bf A}{\bf c})E\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|U=u\right\}\right].

Thus, under Assumption
DD2
with respect to

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\mid\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right\}\quad\text{and}\quad E_{\widetilde{p}}\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\mid\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right\},

(S.37)

contiguity implied by Assumption

DD1
,
E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{4})<\infty
and
E(\|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\|^{2})<\infty

implied by Assumptions
AA1
and
BB1
, using

E\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\right\}=-{\bf I}
and
E\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\}=0
again,
we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}D(\widehat{U}_{i})

(S.38)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\left[({\bf A}-{\bf I})n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}E\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|U=\widehat{U}_{i}\right\}-({\bf A}{\bf c})n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}E\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|U=\widehat{U}_{i}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})\left(E_{\widehat{p}}\left[E\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|\widehat{U}\right\}{\mid\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}\right]+o_{\widehat{p}}(1)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-({\bf A}{\bf c})\left(E_{\widehat{p}}\left[E\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|\widehat{U}\right\}{\mid\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}\right]+o_{\widehat{p}}(1)\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left[({\bf A}-{\bf I})E_{\widehat{p}}\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\mid\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right\}-({\bf A}{\bf c})E_{\widehat{p}}\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\mid\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right\}+o_{\widehat{p}}(n_{1}^{-1/2})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\left\{{\rm tr}\left[({\bf A}-{\bf I})E\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\right\}\right]-E\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\right\}({\bf A}{\bf c})+o_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\left\{-{\rm tr}({\bf A}-{\bf I})+o_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}{\rm tr}\left\{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}+O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1})+o_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left\{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}+o_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2}).

Combining (
S.36
) and (
S.38
), under Assumptions

DD1
,
DD2
with respect to (
S.33
),
(
S.34
), (
S.35
), (
S.37
), and
Assumptions
AA1
,
BB1
,
BB2
, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}B(\widehat{U}_{i})+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}D(\widehat{U}_{i})

(S.39)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left\{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}+o_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left\{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}+o_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2}n_{2}^{1/2}).

Truncation for the remainder

Similar to the difficulties in handling
\mathcal{E}_{1}
, here it is also
hard to control the remainder term
R_{i}
when
1+\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})
is
too
close to 0 in the last term in (
S.32
). We hence resort to a
truncation treatment.
Let the event
\mathcal{E}_{2}
be
\mathcal{E}_{2}=\{1+\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})\geq\epsilon_{n},\forall i\in\{n_{1}+1,\ldots,n\}\}
. We will follow the same logic as
the truncation procedure for
\mathcal{E}_{1}
: we first control the remainder
term under
\mathcal{E}_{2}
, and then further show that the events outside

\mathcal{E}_{2}
are rare enough to be ignored.

Bounding the integral in the remainder term under
\mathcal{E}_{2}

Under
\mathcal{E}_{2}
, the integral part in
R_{i}
’s can be bounded as the
extreme values of
1+\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})
are ruled out. We have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{r}{\{(1-r)(\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})+1)+r\}^{2}}drI(\mathcal{E}_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{r}{\{(1-r)\epsilon_{n}+r\}^{2}}dr

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{r}{\{r(1-\epsilon_{n})+\epsilon_{n}\}^{2}}dr

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\left\{\frac{1}{(1-\epsilon_{n})^{2}}\mbox{log}|r(1-\epsilon_{n})+\epsilon_{n}|+\frac{\epsilon_{n}}{(1-\epsilon_{n})^{2}}\frac{1}{r(1-\epsilon_{n})+\epsilon_{n}}\right\}_{0}^{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\left\{\frac{1}{(1-\epsilon_{n})^{2}}\left(-\mbox{log}|\epsilon_{n}|\right)+\frac{\epsilon_{n}}{(1-\epsilon_{n})^{2}}\left(1-\frac{1}{\epsilon_{n}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\left\{\frac{-\mbox{log}(\epsilon_{n})+\epsilon_{n}-1}{(1-\epsilon_{n})^{2}}\right\}.

Let
\epsilon_{n}=n_{1}^{-k}
where
k\geq 2
, then

\displaystyle\frac{-\mbox{log}(\epsilon_{n})+\epsilon_{n}-1}{(1-\epsilon_{n})^{2}}=\frac{k\mbox{log}(n_{1})+n_{1}^{-k}-1}{(1-n_{1}^{-k})^{2}}\leq 2k\mbox{log}(n_{1}),

so

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{r}{\{(1-r)(\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})+1)+r\}^{2}}drI(\mathcal{E}_{2})\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}2k\mbox{log}(n_{1}).

(S.40)

Expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}

Note that the integral term in the remainder term
in (
S.40
)
is uniformly bounded under
\mathcal{E}_{2}
, we now bound the rest in the
remainder, which is
n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}
.
We divide
\Delta(u)
into five terms and apply Lemma
S.1
as follows:
