\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\leq 5n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left[\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}{\rm tr}\{[({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}]{\bf B}_{1}(\widehat{U}_{i})\}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+5n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left[\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{2}(\widehat{U}_{i})({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}\right]^{2}+5n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left[\frac{2}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}({\bf A}{\bf c})^{\top}{\bf B}_{3}(\widehat{U}_{i})\mbox{vec}({\bf A}-{\bf I})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+5n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}{\rm tr}\left[({\bf A}-{\bf I})E\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|U=\widehat{U}_{i}\right\}\right]^{2}+5n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left[({\bf A}{\bf c})E\left\{{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|U=\widehat{U}_{i}\right\}\right]^{2}.

We analyze the five terms one by one. For the first term, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left[\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}{\rm tr}\{[({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}]{\bf B}_{1}(\widehat{U}_{i})\}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}}\|{\bf B}_{1}(\widehat{U}_{i})\|_{F}^{2}.

We consider the two parts separately. Firstly,

\displaystyle\|({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}={\rm tr}[\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})\}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}[\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}\otimes\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}]=[{\rm tr}\{({\bf A}-{\bf I})({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\}]^{2}=\|{\bf A}-{\bf I}\|_{F}^{4}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-2}).

Then,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}}\|{\bf B}_{1}(\widehat{U}_{i})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left\|\int_{\|{\bf y}\|=\widehat{U}_{i}}\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})(1-t)\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}dtd{\bf y}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left\|E\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\Big|U=\widehat{U}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}E\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\right\|_{F}^{2}\Big|U=\widehat{U}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}E_{\widehat{p}}\left\{E\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\right\|_{F}^{2}\Big|\widehat{U},\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\Big|\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right\}+n_{2}^{1/2}o_{\widehat{p}}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}E_{\widehat{p}}\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\right\|_{F}^{2}\Big|\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]+n_{2}^{1/2}o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}E\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})(1-t)dt\right\|_{F}^{2}\right]+n_{2}^{1/2}o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{4}E\left[\left\|\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\right\}({\bf Y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf Y}^{\top})\right\|_{F}^{2}\right]+n_{2}^{1/2}o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{4}E\left[{\rm tr}\left\{({\bf Y}\otimes{\bf Y})\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\right\}^{\top}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\right\}({\bf Y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf Y}^{\top})\right\}\right]+n_{2}^{1/2}o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{4}E\left\{{\rm tr}({\bf Y}{\bf Y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf Y}{\bf Y}^{\top})\|{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\|_{F}^{2}\right\}+n_{2}^{1/2}o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\frac{1}{4}E\left\{\|{\bf Y}\|^{4}\|{\bf S}_{3}({\bf Y})\|_{F}^{2}\right\}+n_{2}^{1/2}o_{p}(1),

where in the above derivations, we use contiguity due to Assumption
DD1

in the sixth
last equality
and also Assumption
DD2
with respect to

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\mbox{vec}\left\{{\bf S}_{3}({\bf y}_{t})\right\}({\bf y}^{\top}\otimes{\bf y}^{\top})\frac{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{t})}{f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})}(1-t)dt\right\|_{F}^{2}\Big|\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]

(S.41)

Thus, under all assumptions mentioned above, the first term has the order

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left[\frac{1}{f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})}{\rm tr}\{[({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}\otimes({\bf A}-{\bf I})^{\top}]{\bf B}_{1}(\widehat{U}_{i})\}\right]^{2}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-2}n_{2}^{1/2}).

Similarly, for the second term, we have

under Assumptions

,

Also similarly, for the third term, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left({\rm tr}\left[({\bf A}-{\bf I})E\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|U=\widehat{U}_{i}\right\}\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\|{\bf A}-{\bf I}\|_{F}^{2}\left\|E\left\{{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}|U=\widehat{U}_{i}\right\}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\bf A}-{\bf I}\|_{F}^{2}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}E\left\{\|{\bf Y}{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}|U=\widehat{U}_{i}\right\}

\displaystyle\|{\bf A}-{\bf I}\|_{F}^{2}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}E\left\{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}\|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\|^{2}|U=\widehat{U}_{i}\right\}

\displaystyle\|{\bf A}-{\bf I}\|_{F}^{2}n_{2}^{1/2}\left\{E_{\widehat{p}}\left[E\left\{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}\|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\|^{2}|U=\widehat{U}\right\}\mid\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]+o_{\widehat{p}}(1)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\bf A}-{\bf I}\|_{F}^{2}n_{2}^{1/2}\left[E_{\widehat{p}}\left\{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}\|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\|^{2}\mid\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right\}+o_{\widehat{p}}(1)\right]

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1})n_{2}^{1/2}\left[E\left\{\|{\bf Y}\|^{2}\|{\bf S}_{1}({\bf Y})\|^{2}\right\}+o_{p}(1)\right]

,

,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}).

(S.46)

Order of the remainder term under

Inserting (

(S.47)

Analysis of
\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C}

Similar to the case for
\mathcal{E}_{1}
, we now show that the event
\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C}

happens rare enough by estimating the rate of
I(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C})
as follows.

\displaystyle I(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I\left(\exists i\in\{n_{1}+1,\ldots,n\}\mbox{ s.t. }1+\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})<\epsilon_{n}\right)\leq\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}I\left(1+\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})\leq\epsilon_{n}\right)

(S.48)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}I\left(\frac{\epsilon_{n}}{1+\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})}\geq 1\right)\leq\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{\epsilon_{n}}{1+\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})}=\epsilon_{n}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{1}{1+\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}\epsilon_{n}E_{\widehat{p}}\left\{\frac{1}{1+\Delta(\widehat{U})}\Big|\widehat{\bm{\mu}},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\right\}+n_{2}\epsilon_{n}o_{\widehat{p}}(1)=O_{p}(n_{2}\epsilon_{n}),

under contiguity due to Assumption
DD1
, and Assumption
DD2
with respect to

\displaystyle E_{\widetilde{p}}\left\{\frac{1}{1+\Delta(\widetilde{U})}\Big|\widetilde{\bm{\mu}},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\right\}

(S.49)

When
\epsilon=n_{1}^{-k}
, then

\displaystyle I(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C})=O_{p}(n_{2}n_{1}^{-k}),

Summary of results under
\mathcal{E}_{2}

Following from (
S.31
), we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})\}I(\mathcal{E}_{2})

(S.50)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left[\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})-\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{r}{\{(1-r)(\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})+1)+r\}^{2}}dr\right]I(\mathcal{E}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|I(\mathcal{E}_{2})+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})I(\mathcal{E}_{2})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|I(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C})-n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})I(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C}).

By continuous mapping theorem, we have
\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2})
. Thus,

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|I(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C})=n_{2}^{1/2}O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1/2})O_{p}(n_{2}n_{1}^{-k})=O_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k-1/2})

Also, by (
S.39
), we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})I(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C})=O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})O_{p}(n_{2}n_{1}^{-k})=o_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}).

Therefore, under all above conditions, using
(
S.5
) and
(
S.39
), we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})\}I(\mathcal{E}_{2})

(S.51)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}|\det({\bf A})|+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\Delta(\widehat{U}_{i})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))+O_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k-1/2})+o_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\}+O_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1})+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left\{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\right\}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))+o_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))+o_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}).

\mathcal{E}_{2}^{C}

In contrast, under

,

Combining (

S.6.4.2

Asymptotic expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(U_{i})\}

Main expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(U_{i})\}

By Taylor expansion, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{u}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})\}

(S.55)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})+\frac{1}{2}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{2}(u_{i}^{*})(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})^{2},

where
u_{i}^{*}
is between
u_{i}
and
\widehat{u}_{i}
. Note that

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{2}(u_{i}^{*})(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{2}(u_{i})(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})^{2}+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{Q_{2}(u_{i}^{*})-Q_{2}(u_{i})\}(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})^{2}

By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{2}(u_{i})(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\left\{n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{2}(u_{i})^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\left\{n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})^{4}\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\left[E\{Q_{2}(U)^{2}\}+o_{p}(1)\right]^{1/2}\left\{O_{p}(n_{1}^{-2})\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}),

where the second last equality uses Assumptions
CC2
and

E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{4})<\infty
implied by Assumption
AA1
, and
Lemma
S.3
is also applied here. The other term can be bounded
under Assumptions
EE2
and
E(\|{\bf Y}\|^{3})<\infty
implied by
Assumption
AA1
using Lemma
S.3
again, as

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{Q_{2}(u_{i}^{*})-Q_{2}(u_{i})\}(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})^{2}\right|\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|Q_{2}(u_{i}^{*})-Q_{2}(u_{i})|(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}L|u_{i}^{*}-u_{i}|(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})^{2}\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}L\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i}|^{3}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-3/2}n_{2}^{1/2}).

Plugging back into (
S.55
), and using (
S.8
), we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{u}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})\}

(S.56)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})(\widehat{u}_{i}-u_{i})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bf y}_{i}^{\top}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf y}_{j}\}/u_{i}

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})(S_{i4}+S_{i3}+S_{i1}+S_{i2})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}).

Ignorable terms in (
S.56
)

Note that

|S_{i4}|\leq\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|\|{\bf r}_{4}\|
,
|S_{i3}|\leq\|{\bf r}_{3}\|
, so

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})S_{i4}\right|\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|Q_{1}(u_{i})||S_{i4}|\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|Q_{1}(u_{i})|\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|\|{\bf r}_{4}\|

\displaystyle=n_{2}^{1/2}\{E(\|{\bf Y}\||Q_{1}(U)|)+o_{p}(1)\}O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1})=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

under
E(\|{\bf Y}\||Q_{1}(U)|)<\infty
implied by Assumptions
AA1
and
CC1
, and

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})S_{i3}\right|\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|Q_{1}(u_{i})||S_{i3}|\leq n_{2}^{1/2}\{E(|Q_{1}(U)|)+o_{p}(1)\}\|{\bf r}_{3}\|=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

under
E(|Q_{1}(U)|)<\infty
implied by Assumption
CC1
.
We then consider the terms regarding
S_{i1}
and
S_{i2}
.
For the term related to
S_{i1}
, using Lemma
S.4
and (
S.10
), we have

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})S_{i1}\right|\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|Q_{1}(u_{i})||S_{i1}|\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\frac{5}{2}|Q_{1}(u_{i})|\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

under

and

, which are implied by Assumptions

,

and

.
Also, for the term related to
S_{i2}
, using Lemma
S.4
again and (
S.11
), we have

\displaystyle\left|n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})S_{i2}\right|\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|Q_{1}(u_{i})||S_{i2}|\leq n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}|Q_{1}(u_{i})|\frac{\|\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}-{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{3}}{\|{\bf Y}_{i}\|^{2}}=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-3/2}n_{2}^{1/2})

under
E(|Q_{1}(U)|\|{\bf Y}\|)<\infty
and
E(|Q_{1}(U)|/\|{\bf Y}\|^{2})<\infty
.
Here,
E(|Q_{1}(U)|\|{\bf Y}\|)<\infty
is ensured by Assumptions

AA1
and
CC1
, while

E(|Q_{1}(U)|/\|{\bf Y}\|^{2})\leq\{E(|Q_{1}(U)|^{3})\}^{1/3}\{E(1/\|{\bf Y}\|^{3})\}^{2/3}<\infty
is
ensured by Hölder’s inequality,
Assumptions

AA2
and

CC1
.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})(S_{i4}+S_{i3}+S_{i1}+S_{i2})=O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}).

Final expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(U_{i})\}

Continuing from (
S.56
), we have

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{u}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})\}

(S.57)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i})n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\bf y}_{i}^{\top}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf y}_{i}-{\bf y}_{j}\}/u_{i}+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\{n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bf y}_{i}{\bf y}_{i}^{\top}/u_{i}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\}

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}Q_{1}(u_{i}){\bf v}_{i}^{\top}{\bf y}_{j}+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf y}_{j}

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2}).

S.6.4.3

Asymptotic expansion of
\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{u}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})\}

Combining (
S.54
) and (
S.57
),

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(U_{i})\}

(S.58)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})\}+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(\widehat{U}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(U_{i})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}+n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))+o_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf y}_{j}

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}+n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf y}_{j}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}(n_{1})+n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}).

S.6.4.4

Final result for
-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})+H(U)

By (
S.58
) and Section 4 of
Berrett et al. 2019
, we give the final
asymptotic expansion of
-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})+H(U)
as follows:

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\{\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})-H(U)\}

(S.59)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left\{\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})+\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{u}_{i})\right\}-n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\widehat{U}}(\widehat{u}_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})\}

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\left\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})+H(U)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{\widehat{p}}(1)-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf y}_{j}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}(n_{1})+n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k})-n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})+H(U)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf y}_{j}

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})+H(U)\}+o_{p}(1+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\mbox{log}(n_{1})+n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}),

where the last equality also uses contiguity implied by Assumption
DD1
.

S.6.5

Summary

Combining (
5
),
(
S.18
), (
S.29
), (
S.59
), as long as
k\geq 2
, we obtain

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\{T_{1}-d(f_{U,{\bf V}}\|f_{U}f_{0})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}\{-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})\}+n_{2}^{1/2}(p-1)[\widehat{E}_{n_{2}}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}]+n_{2}^{1/2}\{\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})-H(U)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\}+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+H({\bf Y})\}

\displaystyle+n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}(p-1)\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bf y}_{j}\right]

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{-1/2}(p-1)\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(U_{i})-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}\}-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf y}_{j}-n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})+H(U)\}+o_{p}(1+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}+n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}{\rm tr}\left([(p-1)E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right)-{\bf I}-E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}]{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle-n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\left[(p-1)E({\bf V}^{\top}/U)-E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}\right]{\bf y}_{j}

\displaystyle+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+(p-1)\mbox{log}(u_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})\}+n_{2}^{1/2}\{H({\bf Y})-(p-1)E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}-H(U)\}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(1+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})+O_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}+n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}\psi_{1}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\psi_{2}({\bf X}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle+o_{p}(1+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2})+O_{p}(n_{2}^{3/2}n_{1}^{-k}+n_{1}^{-1/2}+n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\mbox{log}(n_{1}))

where

\displaystyle\psi_{1}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\left(\left[(p-1)E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right)-{\bf I}-E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}\right]{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right)

(S.60)

\displaystyle-\left[(p-1)E({\bf V}^{\top}/U)-E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}\right]{\bf y},

\displaystyle\psi_{2}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})+(p-1)\mbox{log}(u)-\mbox{log}f_{U}(u)+H({\bf Y})-(p-1)E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}-H(U).

(S.61)

∎

S.7

Proof of Theorem
3

We consider the test statistics

\displaystyle T_{1}\equiv-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U},\widehat{\bf V})+\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})-\mbox{log}c_{p}=-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+(p-1)\widehat{E}_{n_{2}}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}+\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})-\mbox{log}c_{p}

based on the data
(\widehat{U}_{i},\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}),i=n_{1}+1,\dots,n
.
Similarly, we define

\displaystyle T_{2}\equiv-\widehat{H}_{n_{1}}(\widehat{U},\widehat{\bf V})+\widehat{H}_{n_{1}}(\widehat{U})-\mbox{log}c_{p}=-\widehat{H}_{n_{1}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+(p-1)\widehat{E}_{n_{1}}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}+\widehat{H}_{n_{1}}(\widehat{U})-\mbox{log}c_{p}

based on the data
(\widehat{U}_{i},\widehat{\bf Y}_{i}),i=1,\dots,n_{1}
. Recall the
notations in (
12
) and (
4.2
).
We set
n_{1}=n_{2}=\lfloor n/2\rfloor
. We first consider the
\overline{H}_{n}(\widehat{\bf Y})
term.

\displaystyle n^{1/2}\{-\overline{H}_{n}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{-1/2}\left[n_{2}^{1/2}\{-\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})\}+n_{1}^{1/2}\{-\widehat{H}_{n_{1}}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})\}\right]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2^{-1/2}\left(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}+n_{1}^{1/2}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{j=n_{1}+1}^{n}\right){\rm tr}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\}

\displaystyle+2^{-1/2}\left(n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}+n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{1}}\right)\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+H({\bf Y})\}+O_{p}(n^{-1/2})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n^{-1/2}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}}{\rm tr}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\}+n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+H({\bf Y})\}+O_{p}(n^{-1/2})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{T1}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+O_{p}(n^{-1/2})+o_{p}(1)

where

\displaystyle\psi_{T1}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})=-{\rm tr}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\}+\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y})+H({\bf Y}).

We then consider the
\overline{E}_{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}
term.

\displaystyle n^{1/2}[\overline{E}_{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{-1/2}n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}\}+2^{-1/2}n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{1}}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U}_{i})-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{-1/2}\left(n_{1}^{-1}n_{2}^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}+n_{2}^{-1}n_{1}^{1/2}\sum_{j=n_{1}+1}^{n}\right)\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bf Y}_{j}\right]

\displaystyle+2^{-1/2}\left(n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}+n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{1}}\right)\{\mbox{log}(U_{i})-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}\}+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}\mbox{log}(n)+n^{3/2-k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}}\left[{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bf Y}_{j}\right]

\displaystyle+n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}(U_{i})-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}\}+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}\mbox{log}(n)+n^{3/2-k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{T2}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}\mbox{log}(n)+n^{3/2-k})

where

\displaystyle\psi_{T2}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bf y}+\mbox{log}(u)-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}.

We finally consider the
\overline{H}_{n}(\widehat{U})
term.

\displaystyle n^{1/2}\{\overline{H}_{n}(\widehat{U})-H(U)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{-1/2}\left[n_{2}^{1/2}\{\widehat{H}_{n_{2}}(\widehat{U})-H(U)\}+n_{1}^{1/2}\{\widehat{H}_{n_{1}}(\widehat{U})-H(U)\}\right]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2^{-1/2}\left(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}+n_{1}^{1/2}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{j=n_{1}+1}^{n}\right){\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]

\displaystyle+2^{-1/2}\left(n_{2}^{1/2}n_{1}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{1}}+n_{1}^{1/2}n_{2}^{-1}\sum_{j=n_{1}+1}^{n}\right)E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf y}_{j}

\displaystyle-2^{-1/2}\left(n_{2}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=n_{1}+1}^{n}+n_{1}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{1}}\right)\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})+H(U)\}+o_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n^{3/2-k}+n^{-1/2}\mbox{log}(n))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n^{-1/2}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}}{\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x}_{j},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]+n^{-1/2}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}}E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf y}_{j}

\displaystyle-n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})+H(U)\}+o_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n^{3/2-k}+n^{-1/2}\mbox{log}(n))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{T3}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+o_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n^{3/2-k}+n^{-1/2}\mbox{log}(n))

where

\displaystyle\psi_{T3}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]+E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf y}-\mbox{log}f_{U}(u)-H(U).

Therefore, as long as
k\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle n^{1/2}\{T-d(f_{U,{\bf V}}\|f_{U}f_{0})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{1/2}\{-\overline{H}_{n}(\widehat{\bf Y})+H({\bf Y})+(p-1)\overline{E}_{n}\{\mbox{log}(\widehat{U})\}-(p-1)E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}+\overline{H}_{n}(\widehat{U})-H(U)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\psi_{T1}({\bf X}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+(p-1)\psi_{T2}({\bf X}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+\psi_{T3}({\bf X}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})\}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[-{\rm tr}\{{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\}+\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf Y}_{i})+H({\bf Y})

\displaystyle+(p-1){\rm tr}\left\{E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right){\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right\}-(p-1)E\left({\bf V}^{\top}/U\right){\bf Y}_{i}

\displaystyle+(p-1)[\mbox{log}(U_{i})-E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}]-{\rm tr}[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})]

\displaystyle+E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}{\bf Y}_{i}-\mbox{log}f_{U}(U_{i})-H(U)\Big]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\rm tr}\left(\left[(p-1)E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right)-{\bf I}-E\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\}\right]{\bm{\psi}}_{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}}({\bf X}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}}){\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle-n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[(p-1)E({\bf V}^{\top}/U)-E\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}^{\top}\}]{\bf y}_{i}+n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\mbox{log}f_{\bf Y}({\bf y}_{i})+(p-1)\mbox{log}(u_{i})-\mbox{log}f_{U}(u_{i})\}

\displaystyle+n^{1/2}\{H({\bf Y})-(p-1)E\{\mbox{log}(U)\}-H(U)\}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+o_{p}(1),

where
\psi({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})=\psi_{1}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})+\psi_{2}({\bf x},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})
, as defined in (
S.60
) and (
S.61
).

Under
H_{0}
,
E({\bf V})={\bf 0},E({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top})={\bf I}/p
,
E({\bf V}/U)=E({\bf V})E(1/U)={\bf 0}
, and

\displaystyle E\left\{Q_{1}(U){\bf V}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\left\{Q_{1}(U)\right\}E({\bf V})={\bf 0},

\displaystyle E\left\{Q_{1}(U)U{\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\left\{Q_{1}(U)U\right\}E\left({\bf V}{\bf V}^{\top}\right)=-p^{-1}{\bf I}.

This leads to under
H_{0}
,
n^{1/2}T=o_{p}(1)
, which has the same
as in the known
\mu,{\bm{\Sigma}}
case.
Under
H_{a}
,

\displaystyle n^{1/2}\{T-d(f_{U,{\bf V}}\|f_{U}f_{0})\}\sim N[0,E\{\psi({\bf X},{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\Sigma}})^{2}\}].

∎
